# Canonical multiplicity-space data of gl(4), V_Lambda (x) V_Lambda' with Lambda = {6,4,2,0} (REP, dim DIM) and Lambda' = {6,4,2,0} (REP2, dim DIM2): for EVERY irrep mu the PRV slice space M_mu (exact basis), the three two-site Gaudin missing-label operators V3, V4a, V4b REDUCED TO THE SLICE (exact m x m matrices in the PRV basis), their exact characteristic polynomials, the exact G-Gram of the PRV basis, and the canonical copies (joint eigenvectors; 30 digits): slice, eigenvalue triple, HW residual, G-norm^2.
# FORMAT: one record per line, TAB-separated fields, first field = tag, nested braces = lists (Wolfram InputForm style). p/q = exact rationals (integers without denominator); the COPY / GNORM2 / VEC numbers are decimals with 30 significant digits; HWRESID are Python floats. Lines starting with # are comments. Same conventions as the G1 files ref/gl4-cg-slices-*.txt (GT order, 1-based indices, two sites, slice).
# ORDER OF THE GT BASES, TWO SITES, SLICE: exactly as in ref/gl4-cg-slices-*.txt (order of gl3_reps.gt_patterns, HW state LAST; site 1 = first factor Lambda (nu, slow), site 2 = Lambda' (q, fast); flat index (nu-1)*DIM2 + q; slice = Psi[., q_top] in the weight space V_Lambda[mu-Lambda'] of site 1; GMET1/GMET2 = invariant diagonal metrics, G[HW] = 1).
# THE OPERATORS (WP-G2, ref/gl4-gaudin-ops.txt; the t-free triple of the two-site gl(4) Gaudin model with L(u)_ij = e1_ij/(u-theta1) + e2_ij/(u-theta2), t = theta1 - theta2, cdet(d_u - L) = d^4 - S1 d^3 + S2 d^2 - S3 d + S4 column-ordered): V3 = t S_{3,2}, V4a = t S_{4,3}, V4b = t^2 S_{4,2} (S_{k,j} = Laurent coefficient of S_k at theta1 of order x^-j, x = u - theta1); exactly t-independent, mutually commuting, self-adjoint for G (x) G, simple joint spectrum on every sector tested (G2).
# THE REDUCTION RULE (this file's VRED matrices): a term c W1 W2 of an operator (W1 = word of site-1 generators, W2 = word of site-2 generators) acts on the slice psi_top of a HW vector as follows: normal-order W2 in U(gl_4) as sum c_w L H R (L lowering, H Cartan, R raising factors); a term with L != 1 contributes 0; otherwise c c_w prod_{E_aa in H} Lambda'_a (-1)^|R| W1 E_{r_|R|} ... E_{r_1} psi_top for R = e2_{r_1} ... e2_{r_|R|} (raising factors move to site 1 with the sign (-1)^|R|, in REVERSED order, to the right of W1; every site-2 Cartan factor is evaluated to Lambda'). So each operator acts on M_mu as a polynomial in the site-1 generators of V_Lambda: no site-2 vector, no n_W-sized object.
